- **recall**: Use /augenta:recall [answer | context] &lt;question&gt; in Claude Code or $augenta:recall [answer | context] &lt;question&gt; in Codex. Ask the Augenta Workspaces this project feeds what they remember about a topic, and bring the answer into the conversation. Use when the user runs /augenta:recall or $augenta:recall; asks what was decided, tried, learned, or seen before, why something is the way it is, or what the team or Augenta already knows about something; or when you are about to work on a topic this session has no context for and the project is connected to Augenta.
